## 8. Post-Implementation Checklist

- [x] PC-1 met (sbx `/dev/null` hunk committed by Zoltán before M1: `4d3640c`, M0:15).
- [ ] PC-2 honoured (hunk-level edits, staged with `git apply --cached`; user hunks untouched).
- [ ] Every test group was run RED before implementation; red counts recorded in ADR 0031/0032.
- [ ] Spec acceptance rows `mcp_sandbox:E1-E18` + risk rows `mcp_sandbox:E19-E34` (R3:L3) green on macOS; Linux-only rows INFO; `liveVerified` linux=false.
- [ ] No `(allow file-read* (subpath "/"))`, no global `file-read-metadata`, no unfiltered `process-exec`, no carve-out in any restricted profile (`sbx_restrict:E2-E5`); no `--ro-bind` of a shadow target outside the write scopes in restricted bwrap (`sbx_restrict:F10`); `--read` never realpath'd (`sbx_restrict:B3`, `sbx_restrict:B5`).
- [ ] Legacy sbx output byte-identical without new flags (`sbx_restrict:D4`, `sbx_restrict:F9`); abbreviated flags refused (`sbx_restrict:A15`); `sbx_gate` 105 and `sbx_seccomp` 33 unchanged.
- [ ] Gate flag table (legacy + new flags, `_BOOL_FLAGS | _VALUE_FLAGS | _PROMPT_FLAGS`) equals the sbx parser option set (`sbx_restrict:S14`); relative `--read`/`--exec` prompt (`sbx_restrict:S15`); `--net` still prompts (`sbx_restrict:S13`); `--status-fd` always prompts while `--timeout` is allowed (`sbx_restrict:S16`, `sbx_restrict:S10`; R2:S-L1); above-default rlimits prompt (`sbx_restrict:S11`).
- [ ] sbx installs its SIGTERM/SIGINT/SIGHUP handlers BEFORE `fork()` and the child restores `SIG_DFL` before exec (R2:L3); the legacy exec site is still the literal `os.execvp(argv[0], argv)` (sbx:1285; R2:L9; `sbx_seccomp` 33 green); in `main()`'s source the zero-argument `_apply_rlimits()` still precedes the first `os.execvp(` and a legacy invocation calls no new I/O (`sbx_gate` row Q and `_main_in`, `tests/test_sbx_gate.py:1428-1437`, :350-379; R3:I1).
- [ ] Restricted mode denies ACL writes in the write scope with `file-write-acl` ONLY and never `file-write-flags` (`sbx_restrict:E10`, `sbx_restrict:L11` — `chflags uchg`, `shutil.copy2`/`copytree` still work; M0:8); the 3l STOP condition did not fire (measured, `item-3l.md`; R3:S-M1); a setuid exec is refused by Seatbelt (sh exit 126, `mcp_sandbox:E34`; M0:10); the 100-level depth cap is live with the literal 100-group regex (`sbx_restrict:L12`, `sbx_restrict:E1`; M0:9); `(target same-sandbox)`, realpath exec literals, PATH-gated exec-dir metadata, the restricted `/dev/null` literal and `/private/var/select/sh` on every darwin plan (M0:2-M0:5).
- [ ] Status protocol: `spawned` carries pid + pgid (`sbx_restrict:U2`); exit 124 on timeout (`sbx_restrict:U1`); kill-before-reap via kqueue `NOTE_EXIT` on darwin / `waitid(WNOWAIT)` on Linux (`sbx_restrict:U6`); runner kills the adopted group when sbx dies (`mcp_sandbox:E33`).
- [ ] Strict rlimits: a refused requested limit is exit 3 (`sbx_restrict:V3`); soft == hard (`sbx_restrict:V1-V2`).
- [ ] Engine env is `{}` on both platforms; darwin target env via the `/usr/bin/env -i` trampoline (`sbx_restrict:T4`, `sbx_restrict:T6`, `sbx_restrict:T7`); runner env to sbx is exactly two keys; target env exactly the five keys plus the M0-3h named `ENV_ADDITIONS` (R2:L5); canary + `MCP_PROXY_TOKEN` absent (`mcp_sandbox:D4`, `mcp_sandbox:E16`).
- [ ] Workdir removed after every outcome incl. chmod 000, `0500`/`0100` dirs, nested chmod 000, `chflags uchg`, a deny-`delete` ACL, a broad deny ACE, a 10 000-level tree and a rename-chained deep tree within the 10 s deadline, by the iterative fd-relative FLATTEN remover (cut at <= 50, fd window <= 50, `fchmod(fd, 0o700)` only when `(st_mode & 0o700) != 0o700`, deadline before every mutating op, darwin empty-ACL strip via `acl_set_fd_np` with the `chmodx_np`/`acl_set_file` fallback; M0:8, M0:9), no `shutil.rmtree` (`mcp_sandbox:E17`, `mcp_sandbox:H8`, `mcp_sandbox:H11`-`H14`, `mcp_sandbox:H19`); a workdir that still resists is quarantined and the runner keeps serving (`mcp_sandbox:H15`); `degraded` only past 16 quarantined entries (`mcp_sandbox:H16`); `capabilities` reports the quarantine count (R2:S-H1, R2:M3); the startup sweep runs under ONE shared 20 s deadline and leaves untried entries quarantined (`mcp_sandbox:H17`), the degraded pre-run retry under a fixed 2 s (`mcp_sandbox:H18`) (R3:M4 = S-M2).
- [ ] KD-17 bound enforced, not a target: degraded retry 2 s, refresh deadline 30 s, kill grace 2 s, cleanup deadline 10 s, `max_timeout_ms <= 120000` refused above (`mcp_sandbox:A3`); run bound 174.5 s <= 175 s (R2:L6, R3:M4); startup bound sweep 20 s + plans 75 s = 95 s < the runner proxy's `startup_timeout` 120 (R3:M4); the timing constants live in one block (Step 7); the refresh term is spent at most ONCE per run — one `plan_deadline` passed to `PlanCache.get(name, plan_deadline)` covers a wait and any own refresh (`mcp_sandbox:G13` waiter bound, R4:M2); every `/bin/ps` VSZ sample is bounded by `min(5, remaining)` (R4:L2).
- [ ] Work root single-owner `flock` (`mcp_sandbox:G11`); every test/smoke/footprint config's `work_root` under `.claude/tmp/`.
- [ ] Interpreter plans self-refresh on signature change (`mcp_sandbox:G9`); a failed refresh refuses runs and shows in capabilities (`mcp_sandbox:G10`); the refresh holds a run slot (`mcp_sandbox:G12`); a cancel during a refresh leaves the plan state unchanged and starts no 5 s suppression, and a run waiting on the cancelled refresh answers within one 30 s term (`mcp_sandbox:G13`, R3:L4 = S-L1, R4:M2); G12/G13/I11 driven by the pass-through fixture sbx, H15-H18 in-process with the module global `remove_tree` replaced, no test hook in production code (R3:M5).
- [ ] Framework launcher handled: python3's `Python.app/Contents/MacOS/Python` in `execs`, `Versions/<v>/Python` a closure root (`mcp_sandbox:B19`, `mcp_sandbox:B18`); keg scan targeted and capped by the M0-measured value `_KEG_SCAN_MAX_FILES = 512` (`mcp_sandbox:B20`; M0:11).
- [ ] Engine-stage failure after `spawned` reported as a declared normal result (`mcp_sandbox:I11`, KD-16).
- [ ] Server memory flat on 10 GiB stdout (`mcp_sandbox:E11`); no hang on orphan-held pipes (`mcp_sandbox:E10`).
- [ ] Error handling follows ADR 0010 (top-level `"error"`, codes `busy|sandbox_unavailable|invalid_params|internal`, `log.exception` with a literal) — handler_crash rows for both servers.

- [ ] No `subprocess.run`-style call in `Scripts/mcp-sandbox.py`; every sbx spawn (run, VSZ probe, `--version`, self-test) adopted through the one helper `_spawn_sbx` (`pass_fds`, `start_new_session=True`, `reclaim.adopt(proc)`), `/bin/ps` through `_run_child` (R2:M2); `cancel` KILL check green without a `KILL_EXEMPT` row; `_Reclaim`, `_signal_group`, `_spawn_sbx`, `_run_child` and `McpServer._serve`'s re-raising `except asyncio.CancelledError: reclaim.cancel(loop); raise` arm (reaching `os.killpg`) all present from Step 7, so the KILL row was green in the file's first commit (R3:M1, R3:L5); ONE kill grace `_KILL_GRACE_S = 2.0` for cancel and hard deadline.
